Task:
Diagnose and fix this bug in the sshpeaches Android app: If an OpenSSH terminal session is open, the SSH connection closes in the background while the app is not foregrounded, and the app is later foregrounded via a notification, Activity Manager, launcher, or another resume path, it shows the SSH Terminal Open pane with a completely empty and unusable terminal because no backing terminal/session exists. The user currently has to press Back. On foreground/resume, automatically return to the previous screen before the stale terminal pane is drawn, with no fade, animation, brief flicker, or empty terminal frame. Inspect the existing architecture and lifecycle/navigation handling, implement the smallest robust fix across all relevant foreground entry paths, preserve valid live-session behavior, add or update regression tests, and run appropriate tests. +private fun View.findEditTextDescendant(): EditText? {
+    if (this is EditText) return this
+    if (this !is ViewGroup) return null
+    for (index in 0 until childCount) {
+        val match = getChildAt(index).findEditTextDescendant()
+        if (match != null) return match
+    }
+    return null

+            }
+        }
+
+        composeRule.runOnIdle {
+            val root = composeRule.activity.findViewById<View>(android.R.id.content)
+            val imeBridge = root.findEditTextDescendant()
+                ?: error("Terminal IME bridge was not attached")
+            val inputConnection = imeBridge.onCreateInputConnection(EditorInfo())
+                ?: error("Terminal IME bridge did not create an InputConnection")
+
+            chunks.forEach { chunk ->
+                check(inputConnection.commitText(chunk, 1)) {
+                    "IME rejected committed chunk: $chunk"
+                }
+            }
+            check(inputConnection.deleteSurroundingText(3, 0)) {
+                "IME rejected repeated backspace"
+            }
+        }
+
+        composeRule.runOnIdle {
+            val expected = chunks.joinToString(separator = "")
+                .replace('\n', '\r') + "\u007F\u007F\u007F"
+            val actual = sentPayloads.fold(ByteArray(0)) { combined, payload ->
+                combined + payload
+            }.toString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
+            check(actual == expected) {
+                "Terminal input lost or reordered bytes: expected ${expected.length} chars, " +
+                    "received ${actual.length}"
+            }
+        }
+    }

+            MaterialTheme {

+                    onSendShellBytes = if (useSecondSink) {

+                    }
+                )
+            }
+        }
+
+        composeRule.runOnIdle {
+            check(terminalInputConnection().commitText("before", 1))
+            useSecondSink = true
+        }
+        composeRule.waitForIdle()
+        composeRule.runOnIdle {
+            check(terminalInputConnection().commitText("after", 1))
+        }
+
+        composeRule.runOnIdle {
+            check(firstSink.single().toString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8) == "before")
+            check(secondSink.single().toString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8) == "after") {
+                "Remembered terminal input kept an obsolete send callback"
+            }
+        }
+    }

+    private fun terminalInputConnection(): InputConnection {
+        val root = composeRule.activity.findViewById<View>(android.R.id.content)
+        val imeBridge = root.findEditTextDescendant()
+            ?: error("Terminal IME bridge was not attached")
+        return imeBridge.onCreateInputConnection(EditorInfo())
+            ?: error("Terminal IME bridge did not create an InputConnection")
+    }
+
+    private fun View.findTerminalViewDescendant(): TerminalView? {
+        if (this is TerminalView) return this
+        if (this !is ViewGroup) return null
+        for (index in 0 until childCount) {
+            val match = getChildAt(index).findTerminalViewDescendant()
+            if (match != null) return match
+        }
+        return null
+    }
+
+    private fun TerminalView.performSelectionAction(copy: Boolean) {
+        val controllerField = TerminalView::class.java
+            .getDeclaredField("mTextSelectionCursorController")
+            .apply { isAccessible = true }
+        val controller = controllerField.get(this) as? TextSelectionCursorController
+            ?: error("Terminal selection controller was not created")

+        check(controller.actionMode.menu.performIdentifierAction(actionId, 0)) {

+    private fun startMoshInputWriter(
+        hostId: String,
+        inputQueue: BoundedMoshInputQueue
+    ): Job = serviceScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
+        while (isActive) {
+            val payload = inputQueue.take() ?: break
+            var offset = 0
+            while (offset < payload.size && isActive) {
+                val session = activeConnections[hostId]?.moshBinding?.session
+                if (session == null || !session.isRunning) {
+                    delay(MOSH_INPUT_RETRY_DELAY_MS)
+                    continue
+                }
+                val chunkSize = min(MOSH_INPUT_CHUNK_BYTES, payload.size - offset)
+                val wrote = session.writeWithTimeout(
+                    payload,
+                    offset,
+                    chunkSize,
+                    MOSH_INPUT_WRITE_TIMEOUT_MS
+                )
+                if (wrote) {
+                    offset += chunkSize
+                } else {
+                    delay(MOSH_INPUT_RETRY_DELAY_MS)
+                }
+            }
+            if (offset == payload.size) {
+                emitShellStreamDiagnostic(hostId = hostId, direction = "TX", payload = payload, size = payload.size)
+            }
+        }
+    }

+            private fun protectedRange(): IntRange {
+                val selectionStart = Selection.getSelectionStart(shadow).coerceIn(0, shadow.length)
+                val selectionEnd = Selection.getSelectionEnd(shadow).coerceIn(0, shadow.length)
+                var start = minOf(selectionStart, selectionEnd)
+                var end = maxOf(selectionStart, selectionEnd)
+                val composingStart = getComposingSpanStart(shadow)
+                val composingEnd = getComposingSpanEnd(shadow)
+                if (composingStart >= 0 && composingEnd >= 0) {
+                    start = minOf(start, composingStart, composingEnd)
+                    end = maxOf(end, composingStart, composingEnd)
+                }
+                return start..end
+            }
+
+            private fun flushPendingText() {
+                val pending = shadow.toString()
+                removeComposingSpans(shadow)
+                shadow.clear()
+                Selection.setSelection(shadow, 0)
+                reportSelection()
+                if (pending.isNotEmpty()) {
+                    onCommittedText?.invoke(pending)
+                }
+            }
+
+            private fun reportSelection() {
+                val selectionStart = Selection.getSelectionStart(shadow).coerceAtLeast(0)
+                val selectionEnd = Selection.getSelectionEnd(shadow).coerceAtLeast(0)
+                inputMethodManager()?.updateSelection(
+                    this@TerminalImeBridgeEditText,
+                    selectionStart,
+                    selectionEnd,
+                    getComposingSpanStart(shadow),
+                    getComposingSpanEnd(shadow)
+                )
+            }
         }

    99	    private val connection = object : ServiceConnection {
   100	        override fun onServiceConnected(name: ComponentName?, binder: IBinder?) {
   101	            val service = (binder as? SessionService.SessionBinder)?.getService()
   102	            sessionServiceState.value = service
   103	            serviceBound = true
   104	            serviceConnectionRequested = true
   105	            if (service != null && pendingSftpDirectoryRequests.isNotEmpty()) {
   106	                pendingSftpDirectoryRequests.forEach { (hostId, path) ->
   107	                    service.listSftpDirectory(hostId, path)
   108	                }
   109	                pendingSftpDirectoryRequests.clear()
   110	            }
   111	            UiDebugLog.result("SessionService.onServiceConnected", true, "bound=true")
   112	        }
   113	
   114	        override fun onServiceDisconnected(name: ComponentName?) {
   115	            sessionServiceState.value = null
   116	            serviceBound = false
   117	            serviceConnectionRequested = false
   118	            UiDebugLog.result("SessionService.onServiceDisconnected", true, "bound=false")
   119	        }
   120	    }

   122	    private fun setupBiometricPrompt() {
   123	        UiDebugLog.action("setupBiometricPrompt")
   124	        val manager = BiometricManager.from(this)
   125	        biometricAvailable = manager.canAuthenticate(BiometricManager.Authenticators.BIOMETRIC_STRONG) == BiometricManager.BIOMETRIC_SUCCESS
   126	        if (!biometricAvailable) {
   127	            biometricPrompt = null
   128	            biometricPromptInfo = null

   138	            }
   139	        })

   141	            .setTitle("Unlock SSHPeaches")
   142	            .setSubtitle("Authenticate to continue")
   143	            .setNegativeButtonText("Use PIN")

   148	    private fun requestNotificationPermissionIfNeeded() {
   149	        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) return
   150	        val granted = ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(
   151	            this,
   152	            Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS
   153	        ) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
   154	        if (granted) {
   155	            UiDebugLog.result("notificationPermissionCheck", true, "already-granted")
   156	            return
   157	        }
   158	        markNotificationPermissionRequested()
   159	        UiDebugLog.action("notificationPermissionRequest", "requesting=true")
   160	        notificationPermissionLauncher.launch(Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS)
   161	    }

   175	    private fun buildCorePermissionStatuses(): List<CorePermissionStatus> {
   176	        val notificationsEnabled = NotificationManagerCompat.from(this).areNotificationsEnabled()
   177	        val postNotificationsGranted = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
   178	            ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(
   179	                this,
   180	                Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS
   181	            ) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
   182	        } else {
   183	            true
   184	        }
   185	        val notificationsGranted = postNotificationsGranted && notificationsEnabled
   186	        val notificationsRemediation = notificationPermissionRemediation(postNotificationsGranted)
   187	
   188	        val foregroundServiceGranted = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.P) {
   189	            ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(
   190	                this,
   191	                FOREGROUND_SERVICE_PERMISSION
   192	            ) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
   193	        } else {
   194	            true
   195	        }
   196	
   197	        val foregroundServiceTypeGranted = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.UPSIDE_DOWN_CAKE) {
   198	            val dataSyncGranted = ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(
   199	                this,
   200	                Manifest.permission.FOREGROUND_SERVICE_DATA_SYNC
   201	            ) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
   202	            dataSyncGranted
   203	        } else {
   204	            true
   205	        }
   206	
   207	        return listOf(
   208	            CorePermissionStatus(
   209	                id = "notifications",
   210	                title = "Notifications",
   211	                description = "Required for active SSH session foreground service controls and status.",
   212	                granted = notificationsGranted,
   213	                remediation = notificationsRemediation
   214	            ),
   215	            CorePermissionStatus(
   216	                id = "foreground_service",
   217	                title = "Foreground Service",
   218	                description = "Required to keep SSH sessions alive in background mode.",
   219	                granted = foregroundServiceGranted && foregroundServiceTypeGranted,
   220	                remediation = CorePermissionRemediation.SETTINGS
   221	            )
   222	        )
   223	    }
   224	
   225	    private fun notificationPermissionRemediation(postNotificationsGranted: Boolean): CorePermissionRemediation {
   226	        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU || postNotificationsGranted) {
   227	            return CorePermissionRemediation.REQUEST
   228	        }
   229	        if (shouldShowRequestPermissionRationale(Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS)) {
   230	            return CorePermissionRemediation.REQUEST
   231	        }
   232	        return if (wasNotificationPermissionRequested()) {
   233	            CorePermissionRemediation.SETTINGS
   234	        } else {
   235	            CorePermissionRemediation.REQUEST
   236	        }
   237	    }
   238	
   239	    private fun wasNotificationPermissionRequested(): Boolean =
   240	        getSharedPreferences(CORE_PERMISSION_PREFS, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
   241	            .getBoolean(KEY_NOTIFICATION_PERMISSION_REQUESTED, false)
   242	
   243	    private fun markNotificationPermissionRequested() {
   244	        getSharedPreferences(CORE_PERMISSION_PREFS, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
   245	            .edit {
   246	                putBoolean(KEY_NOTIFICATION_PERMISSION_REQUESTED, true)
   247	            }
   248	    }

   810	    fun markHostUsage(host: HostConnection) {
   811	        onMarkHostUsed(host.id)
   812	        host.preferredIdentityId?.let(onMarkIdentityUsed)
   813	        listOfNotNull(host.preferredForwardId)
   814	            .plus(host.attachedForwards)
   815	            .distinct()
   816	            .forEach(onMarkPortForwardUsed)
   817	    }
   818	
   819	    fun markRequestUsage(request: QuickConnectRequest) {
   820	        request.savedHostId?.let(onMarkHostUsed)
   821	        val savedHost = request.savedHostId?.let { hostId ->
   822	            uiState.hosts.firstOrNull { it.id == hostId }
   823	        }
   824	        savedHost?.preferredIdentityId?.let(onMarkIdentityUsed)
   825	        listOfNotNull(request.forwardId, savedHost?.preferredForwardId)
   826	            .plus(savedHost?.attachedForwards.orEmpty())
   827	            .distinct()
   828	            .forEach(onMarkPortForwardUsed)
   829	    }

   831	    fun openOrStartSavedHostSession(
   832	        host: HostConnection,
   833	        mode: ConnectionMode,
   834	        password: String?,
   835	        fileTransferEntryMode: FileTransferEntryMode? = null
   836	    ) {
   837	        markHostUsage(host)
   838	        val sessionId = sessionIdFor(host.id, mode)
   839	        val existingSnapshot = sessions.firstOrNull { it.hostId == sessionId }
   840	        if (
   841	            existingSnapshot?.status ==
   842	            com.majordaftapps.sshpeaches.app.service.SessionService.SessionStatus.CONNECTING ||
   843	            existingSnapshot?.status ==
   844	            com.majordaftapps.sshpeaches.app.service.SessionService.SessionStatus.ACTIVE
   845	        ) {
   846	            quickConnectRequest.value = quickRequestFromSnapshot(
   847	                existingSnapshot,
   848	                fileTransferEntryMode ?: FileTransferEntryMode.DOWNLOAD
   849	            )
   850	            quickConnectState.value = quickStateFromSnapshot(existingSnapshot, existingSnapshot.host)
   851	            pendingConnectingNavigation.value = false
   852	        } else {
   853	            if (existingSnapshot != null) {
   854	                onStopSession(sessionId)
   855	            }
   856	            resetSessionLogs(sessionId)
   857	            pendingConnectingNavigation.value = true
   858	            quickConnectRequest.value = QuickConnectRequest(
   859	                sessionId = sessionId,
   860	                name = host.name,
   861	                host = host.host,
   862	                port = host.port,
   863	                username = host.username,
   864	                auth = host.preferredAuth,
   865	                mode = mode,
   866	                savedHostId = host.id,
   867	                useMosh = host.useMosh,
   868	                preferredIdentityId = host.preferredIdentityId,
   869	                forwardId = host.preferredForwardId,
   870	                script = host.startupScript,
   871	                terminalProfileId = host.terminalProfileId,
   872	                initialFileTransferEntryMode = fileTransferEntryMode ?: FileTransferEntryMode.DOWNLOAD
   873	            )
   874	            quickConnectState.value = QuickConnectUiState(
   875	                phase = QuickConnectPhase.CONNECTING,
   876	                message = "Connecting to ${host.host}:${host.port}..."
   877	            )
   878	            onStartSession(sessionId, host, mode, password, true)
   879	        }
   880	        scope.launch {
   881	            drawerState.close()
   882	            navController.navigate(
   883	                existingSnapshot?.let(::routeForSnapshot) ?: Routes.CONNECTING
   884	            ) {
   885	                launchSingleTop = true
   886	            }
   887	        }
   888	    }

   973	                }

   976	                }
   977	            }
   978	        }

   498	    fun quickStateFromSnapshot(
   499	        snapshot: com.majordaftapps.sshpeaches.app.service.SessionService.SessionSnapshot,
   500	        host: HostConnection
   501	    ): QuickConnectUiState = when (snapshot.status) {
   502	        com.majordaftapps.sshpeaches.app.service.SessionService.SessionStatus.CONNECTING -> {
   503	            QuickConnectUiState(
   504	                phase = QuickConnectPhase.CONNECTING,
   505	                message = snapshot.statusMessage ?: "Connecting to ${host.host}:${host.port}..."
   506	            )
   507	        }
   508	
   509	        com.majordaftapps.sshpeaches.app.service.SessionService.SessionStatus.ACTIVE -> {
   510	            QuickConnectUiState(
   511	                phase = QuickConnectPhase.SUCCESS,
   512	                message = snapshot.statusMessage ?: "Connected successfully"
   513	            )
   514	        }
   515	
   516	        com.majordaftapps.sshpeaches.app.service.SessionService.SessionStatus.ERROR -> {
   517	            QuickConnectUiState(
   518	                phase = QuickConnectPhase.ERROR,
   519	                message = snapshot.statusMessage ?: "Connection failed"
   520	            )
   521	        }
   522	    }
   523	
   524	    fun quickRequestFromSnapshot(
   525	        snapshot: com.majordaftapps.sshpeaches.app.service.SessionService.SessionSnapshot,
   526	        fileTransferEntryMode: FileTransferEntryMode = FileTransferEntryMode.DOWNLOAD
   527	    ): QuickConnectRequest {
   528	        val host = snapshot.host
   529	        val savedHostId = uiState.hosts.firstOrNull { it.id == host.id }?.id
   530	        return QuickConnectRequest(
   531	            sessionId = snapshot.hostId,
   532	            name = host.name,
   533	            host = host.host,
   534	            port = host.port,
   535	            username = host.username,
   536	            auth = host.preferredAuth,
   537	            mode = snapshot.mode,
   538	            savedHostId = savedHostId,
   539	            useMosh = host.useMosh,
   540	            preferredIdentityId = host.preferredIdentityId,
   541	            forwardId = host.preferredForwardId,
   542	            script = host.startupScript,
   543	            terminalProfileId = host.terminalProfileId,
   544	            initialFileTransferEntryMode = fileTransferEntryMode
   545	        )
   546	    }
   547	
   548	    fun reconcileQuickConnectRequest(
   549	        current: QuickConnectRequest,
   550	        snapshot: SessionSnapshot
   551	    ): QuickConnectRequest {
   552	        val host = snapshot.host
   553	        val savedHostId = uiState.hosts.firstOrNull { it.id == host.id }?.id ?: current.savedHostId
   554	        return current.copy(
   555	            name = host.name,
   556	            host = host.host,
   557	            port = host.port,
   558	            username = host.username,
   559	            auth = host.preferredAuth,
   560	            mode = snapshot.mode,
   561	            savedHostId = savedHostId,
   562	            useMosh = host.useMosh,
   563	            preferredIdentityId = host.preferredIdentityId,
   564	            forwardId = host.preferredForwardId,
   565	            script = host.startupScript,
   566	            terminalProfileId = host.terminalProfileId
   567	        )
   568	    }
   569	
   570	    fun navigateBackFromConnecting() {
   571	        pendingConnectingNavigation.value = false
   572	        val popped = navController.popBackStack()
   573	        if (!popped) {
   574	            val destination = routeBeforeConnecting.value
   575	                .takeIf { it != Routes.CONNECTING && it != Routes.SESSION }
   576	                ?: Routes.HOME
   577	            navController.navigate(destination) {
   578	                popUpTo(Routes.HOME)
   579	            }
   580	        }
   581	    }
   582	
   583	    val currentQuickConnectSnapshot = rawQuickConnectRequest?.let { current ->
   584	        sessions.firstOrNull { it.hostId == current.sessionId }
   585	    }
   586	    val effectiveQuickConnectRequest = when {
   587	        rawQuickConnectRequest != null && currentQuickConnectSnapshot != null -> {
   588	            reconcileQuickConnectRequest(rawQuickConnectRequest, currentQuickConnectSnapshot)
   589	        }
   590	
   591	        else -> rawQuickConnectRequest
   592	    }
   593	    val effectiveQuickConnectState = currentQuickConnectSnapshot?.let { snapshot ->
   594	        quickStateFromSnapshot(snapshot, snapshot.host)
   595	    } ?: quickConnectState.value
   596	    val isSessionVerticalRoute =
   597	        currentRoute == Routes.CONNECTING || currentRoute == Routes.SESSION
   598	
   599	    fun routeForSnapshot(snapshot: SessionSnapshot): String =
   600	        if (snapshot.status == com.majordaftapps.sshpeaches.app.service.SessionService.SessionStatus.ACTIVE) {
   601	            Routes.SESSION
   602	        } else {
   603	            Routes.CONNECTING
   604	        }
   605	
   606	    BackHandler(enabled = drawerState.isOpen) {
   607	        scope.launch { drawerState.close() }
   608	    }
   609	    BackHandler(enabled = isSessionVerticalRoute && !drawerState.isOpen) {
   610	        navigateBackFromConnecting()
   611	    }
   612	    val activeSessionRequest = effectiveQuickConnectRequest
   613	    val currentTitle = when (currentRoute) {
   614	        Routes.HOME -> "Home"
   615	        Routes.CONNECTING,
   616	        Routes.SESSION -> activeSessionRequest?.name?.ifBlank {
   617	            activeSessionRequest.host
   618	        } ?: "Connecting"
   619	        Routes.HELP -> "Help"
   620	        Routes.HOSTS -> "Hosts"
   621	        Routes.UPTIME -> "Uptime"
   622	        Routes.IDENTITIES -> "Identities"
   623	        Routes.FORWARDS -> "Port Forwards"
   624	        Routes.SNIPPETS -> "Snippets"
   625	        Routes.SNIPPET_EDITOR_ROUTE -> "Snippet Editor"
   626	        Routes.KEYBOARD -> "Keyboard Editor"
   627	        Routes.THEME_EDITOR -> "Theme Editor"
   628	        Routes.THEME_EDITOR_EDIT_ROUTE -> "Theme Editor"
   629	        Routes.SETTINGS -> "Settings"
   630	        Routes.OPEN_SOURCE_LICENSES -> "Open Source Licenses"
   631	        else -> "SSHPeaches"
   632	    }
   633	
   634	    LaunchedEffect(uiState.isLocked) {
   635	        if (uiState.isLocked) {
   636	            showQuickConnect.value = false
   637	            showAbout.value = false
   638	        }
   639	    }
   640	
   641	    LaunchedEffect(currentRoute) {
   642	        if (!isSessionVerticalRoute) {
   643	            routeBeforeConnecting.value = currentRoute
   644	        }
   645	    }
   646	
   647	    LaunchedEffect(requestedOpenSessionId, requestedOpenSessionFileTransferEntryMode, sessions, currentRoute) {
   648	        val targetHostId = requestedOpenSessionId ?: return@LaunchedEffect
   649	        val snapshot = sessions.firstOrNull { it.hostId == targetHostId } ?: return@LaunchedEffect
   650	        val host = snapshot.host
   651	        val targetRoute = routeForSnapshot(snapshot)
   652	        quickConnectRequest.value = quickRequestFromSnapshot(
   653	            snapshot,
   654	            requestedOpenSessionFileTransferEntryMode ?: FileTransferEntryMode.DOWNLOAD
   655	        )
   656	        quickConnectState.value = quickStateFromSnapshot(snapshot, host)
   657	        pendingConnectingNavigation.value = false
   658	        autoResumeHandled.value = true
   659	        if (currentRoute != targetRoute) {
   660	            navController.navigate(targetRoute) {
   661	                popUpTo(Routes.HOME)
   662	            }
   663	        }
   664	        onOpenSessionRequestHandled()
   665	    }
   666	
   667	    LaunchedEffect(requestedStartupRoute, backStackEntry?.destination?.route) {
   668	        val startupRoute = requestedStartupRoute ?: return@LaunchedEffect
   669	        if (backStackEntry == null) return@LaunchedEffect
   670	        if (startupRoute != currentRoute && startupRoute != Routes.CONNECTING) {
   671	            navController.navigate(startupRoute) {
   672	                popUpTo(Routes.HOME)
   673	                launchSingleTop = true
   674	            }
   675	        }
   676	        onStartupRouteHandled()
   677	    }
   678	
   679	    LaunchedEffect(quickConnectRequest.value?.sessionId) {
   680	        sawSnapshotForCurrentRequest.value = false
   681	        connectedHostBarCollapsed.value = false
   682	    }
   683	
   684	    LaunchedEffect(rawQuickConnectRequest, currentQuickConnectSnapshot) {
   685	        val request = rawQuickConnectRequest ?: return@LaunchedEffect
   686	        val snapshot = currentQuickConnectSnapshot ?: return@LaunchedEffect
   687	        val reconciledRequest = reconcileQuickConnectRequest(request, snapshot)
   688	        if (quickConnectRequest.value != reconciledRequest) {
   689	            quickConnectRequest.value = reconciledRequest
   690	        }
   691	        val reconciledState = quickStateFromSnapshot(snapshot, snapshot.host)
   692	        if (quickConnectState.value != reconciledState) {
   693	            quickConnectState.value = reconciledState
   694	        }
   695	    }
   696	
   697	    LaunchedEffect(currentQuickConnectSnapshot?.status, currentRoute) {
   698	        val snapshot = currentQuickConnectSnapshot ?: return@LaunchedEffect
   699	        when {
   700	            snapshot.status == com.majordaftapps.sshpeaches.app.service.SessionService.SessionStatus.ACTIVE &&
   701	                currentRoute == Routes.CONNECTING -> {
   702	                navController.navigate(Routes.SESSION) {
   703	                    popUpTo(Routes.CONNECTING) { inclusive = true }
   704	                    launchSingleTop = true
   705	                }
   706	            }
   707	
   708	            snapshot.status != com.majordaftapps.sshpeaches.app.service.SessionService.SessionStatus.ACTIVE &&
   709	                currentRoute == Routes.SESSION -> {
   710	                navController.navigate(Routes.CONNECTING) {
   711	                    popUpTo(Routes.SESSION) { inclusive = true }
   712	                    launchSingleTop = true
   713	                }
   714	            }
   715	        }
   716	    }
   717	
   718	    LaunchedEffect(quickConnectRequest.value?.sessionId, sessions, currentRoute) {
   719	        val request = quickConnectRequest.value ?: return@LaunchedEffect
   720	        val snapshot = sessions.firstOrNull { it.hostId == request.sessionId }
   721	        when (snapshot?.status) {
   722	            null -> {
   723	                if (sawSnapshotForCurrentRequest.value) {
   724	                    pendingConnectingNavigation.value = false
   725	                    quickConnectRequest.value = null
   726	                    quickConnectState.value = QuickConnectUiState()
   727	                    if (isSessionVerticalRoute) {
   728	                        val destination = routeBeforeConnecting.value
   729	                            .takeIf { it != Routes.CONNECTING && it != Routes.SESSION }
   730	                            ?: Routes.HOME
   731	                        navController.navigate(destination) {
   732	                            popUpTo(Routes.HOME)
   733	                        }
   734	                    }
   735	                } else {
   736	                    quickConnectState.value = QuickConnectUiState(
   737	                        phase = QuickConnectPhase.CONNECTING,
   738	                        message = "Connecting to ${request.host}:${request.port}..."
   739	                    )
   740	                }
   741	            }
   742	
   743	            com.majordaftapps.sshpeaches.app.service.SessionService.SessionStatus.CONNECTING -> {
   744	                sawSnapshotForCurrentRequest.value = true
   745	                quickConnectState.value = QuickConnectUiState(
   746	                    phase = QuickConnectPhase.CONNECTING,
   747	                    message = snapshot.statusMessage ?: "Connecting to ${request.host}:${request.port}..."
   748	                )
   749	            }
   750	
   751	            com.majordaftapps.sshpeaches.app.service.SessionService.SessionStatus.ACTIVE -> {
   752	                sawSnapshotForCurrentRequest.value = true
   753	                quickConnectState.value = QuickConnectUiState(
   754	                    phase = QuickConnectPhase.SUCCESS,
   755	                    message = snapshot.statusMessage ?: "Connected successfully"
   756	                )
   757	            }
   758	
   759	            com.majordaftapps.sshpeaches.app.service.SessionService.SessionStatus.ERROR -> {
   760	                sawSnapshotForCurrentRequest.value = true

exec

  1266	    fun closeCurrentConnectingSession() {
  1267	        val request = quickConnectRequest.value
  1268	        if (request != null) {
  1269	            onStopSession(request.sessionId)
  1270	        }
  1271	        pendingConnectingNavigation.value = false
  1272	        val destination = routeBeforeConnecting.value
  1273	            .takeIf { it != Routes.CONNECTING && it != Routes.SESSION }
  1274	            ?: Routes.HOME
  1275	        suppressHomeWelcomeOnReturn.value = destination == Routes.HOME
  1276	        navController.navigate(destination) {
  1277	            popUpTo(Routes.HOME)
  1278	        }
  1279	        quickConnectRequest.value = null
  1280	        quickConnectState.value = QuickConnectUiState()
  1281	    }

  1284	    fun SessionVerticalContent() {
  1285	        val showConnectingTopBar =
  1286	            quickConnectState.value.phase != QuickConnectPhase.CONNECTING &&
  1287	                !connectedHostBarCollapsed.value
  1288	        val showSessionTopBar =
  1289	            currentRoute != Routes.SESSION || !connectedHostBarCollapsed.value
  1290	        val showTopBarForCurrentSessionRoute = when (currentRoute) {
  1291	            Routes.CONNECTING -> showConnectingTopBar
  1292	            Routes.SESSION -> showSessionTopBar
  1293	            else -> false
  1294	        }
  1295	        val request = effectiveQuickConnectRequest
  1296	        val screenState = when {
  1297	            currentRoute == Routes.SESSION && request != null -> {
  1298	                effectiveQuickConnectState.copy(
  1299	                    phase = QuickConnectPhase.SUCCESS,
  1300	                    message = effectiveQuickConnectState.message.ifBlank { "Connected successfully" }
  1301	                )
  1302	            }
  1303	
  1304	            else -> effectiveQuickConnectState
  1305	        }
  1306	        val logs = request?.let { current ->
  1307	            sessionLogs.filter { it.hostId == current.sessionId }
  1308	        } ?: emptyList()
  1309	        val shellOutput = request?.let { current ->
  1310	            shellOutputs[current.sessionId].orEmpty()
  1311	        }.orEmpty()
  1312	        val remoteDirectory = request?.let { current ->
  1313	            remoteDirectories[current.sessionId]
  1314	        }
  1315	        val activeFileTransfer = request?.let { current ->
  1316	            fileTransferProgresses[current.sessionId]
  1317	        }
  1318	        val activeTerminalProfile = uiState.terminalProfiles.firstOrNull {
  1319	            it.id == request?.terminalProfileId
  1320	        } ?: uiState.terminalProfiles.firstOrNull {
  1321	            it.id == uiState.defaultTerminalProfileId
  1322	        } ?: uiState.terminalProfiles.firstOrNull()
  1323	            ?: TerminalProfileDefaults.builtInProfiles.first()
  1324	        ConnectingScreen(
  1325	            request = request,
  1326	            state = screenState,
  1327	            logs = logs,
  1328	            shellOutput = shellOutput,
  1329	            remoteDirectory = remoteDirectory,
  1330	            activeFileTransfer = activeFileTransfer,
  1331	            terminalProfile = activeTerminalProfile,
  1332	            terminalSelectionMode = uiState.terminalSelectionMode,
  1333	            terminalBellMode = uiState.terminalBellMode,
  1334	            diagnosticsLoggingEnabled = uiState.diagnosticsLoggingEnabled,
  1335	            useVolumeButtonsToAdjustFontSize = uiState.terminalVolumeButtonsAdjustFontSize,
  1336	            terminalMarginPx = uiState.terminalMarginPx,
  1337	            useBuiltInKeyboard = uiState.useBuiltInKeyboard,
  1338	            keyboardSlots = uiState.keyboardSlots,
  1339	            snippets = uiState.snippets,
  1340	            onSendShellBytes = { payload ->
  1341	                request?.let { current ->
  1342	                    onSendShellBytes(current.sessionId, payload)
  1343	                }
  1344	            },
  1345	            onTerminalResize = { cols, rows ->
  1346	                request?.let { current ->
  1347	                    onResizeShell(current.sessionId, cols, rows)
  1348	                }
  1349	            },
  1350	            onSftpListDirectory = { path ->
  1351	                request?.let { current ->

  1453	    val updatedGlobalShortcutHandler = rememberUpdatedState<(KeyEvent) -> Boolean> { event ->
  1454	        if (uiState.isLocked || isSessionVerticalRoute) return@rememberUpdatedState false
  1455	        if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN || event.repeatCount > 0) return@rememberUpdatedState false
  1456	
  1457	        when {
  1458	            event.isCtrlPressed && !event.isAltPressed && event.keyCode == KeyEvent.KEYCODE_K -> {
  1459	                showQuickConnect.value = true
  1460	                true
  1461	            }
  1462	
  1463	            event.isCtrlPressed && !event.isAltPressed && event.keyCode == KeyEvent.KEYCODE_SLASH -> {
  1464	                showShortcutHelp.value = true
  1465	                true
  1466	            }
  1467	
  1468	            event.keyCode == KeyEvent.KEYCODE_F1 -> {
  1469	                drawerDestinations.firstOrNull { it.route == Routes.HELP }?.let(onDrawerDestinationSelected)
  1470	                true
  1471	            }
  1472	
  1473	            event.isAltPressed -> {
  1474	                val shortcutRoute = when (event.keyCode) {
  1475	                    KeyEvent.KEYCODE_1 -> Routes.HOME
  1476	                    KeyEvent.KEYCODE_2 -> Routes.HOSTS
  1477	                    KeyEvent.KEYCODE_3 -> Routes.UPTIME
  1478	                    KeyEvent.KEYCODE_4 -> Routes.IDENTITIES
  1479	                    KeyEvent.KEYCODE_5 -> Routes.FORWARDS
  1480	                    KeyEvent.KEYCODE_6 -> Routes.SNIPPETS
  1481	                    KeyEvent.KEYCODE_7 -> Routes.KEYBOARD
  1482	                    KeyEvent.KEYCODE_8 -> Routes.THEME_EDITOR
  1483	                    KeyEvent.KEYCODE_9 -> Routes.SETTINGS
  1484	                    else -> null
  1485	                }
  1486	                if (shortcutRoute != null) {
  1487	                    drawerDestinations.firstOrNull { it.route == shortcutRoute }?.let(onDrawerDestinationSelected)
  1488	                    true
  1489	                } else {
  1490	                    false
  1491	                }
  1492	            }
  1493	
  1494	            else -> false
  1495	        }
  1496	    }

   708	        backgroundSessionTimeoutJob = null

   713	        serviceConnectionRequested = false
   714	        super.onDestroy()

   716	    }

   731	    override fun onStart() {
   732	        super.onStart()
   733	        backgroundSessionTimeoutJob?.cancel()
   734	        backgroundSessionTimeoutJob = null
   735	        if (appUiBootstrapped) {
   736	            appViewModel.onAppForegrounded()
   737	        }
   738	        window.decorView.post {
   739	            ensureSessionServiceConnection()
   740	        }
   741	    }

   745	        refreshCorePermissionStatuses()
   746	    }
   747	
   748	    override fun onStop() {
   749	        super.onStop()
   750	        backgroundSessionTimeoutJob?.cancel()
   751	        backgroundSessionTimeoutJob = null
   752	        if (isChangingConfigurations || !appUiBootstrapped) {
   753	            UiDebugLog.action("MainActivity.onStop", "persistSessions=true (config-change-or-not-ready)")
   754	            UiDebugLog.result("MainActivity.onStop", true, "stoppedAllSessions=false")
   755	            return
   756	        }
   757	        appViewModel.onAppBackgrounded()
   758	        val keepSessionsForActiveForwards = sessionServiceState.value?.hasActivePortForwards() == true
   759	        when {
   760	            keepSessionsForActiveForwards -> {
   761	                UiDebugLog.result(
   762	                    "MainActivity.backgroundSessionTimeout",
   763	                    true,
   764	                    "stopNow=skip-active-port-forwards"
   765	                )
   766	            }
   767	            !latestAllowBackgroundSessions -> {
   768	                sessionServiceState.value?.stopAllSessions()
   769	                UiDebugLog.result("MainActivity.backgroundSessionTimeout", true, "stopNow=background-disabled")
   770	            }
   771	            latestBackgroundSessionTimeout.durationMillis != null -> {
   772	                val timeoutMs = latestBackgroundSessionTimeout.durationMillis ?: 0L
   773	                backgroundSessionTimeoutJob = lifecycleScope.launch {
   774	                    delay(timeoutMs)
   775	                    sessionServiceState.value?.stopAllSessions()
   776	                    UiDebugLog.result(
   777	                        "MainActivity.backgroundSessionTimeout",
   778	                        true,
   779	                        "stopNow=timer elapsedMs=$timeoutMs"
   780	                    )
   781	                }
   782	            }
   783	            else -> {
   784	                UiDebugLog.result("MainActivity.backgroundSessionTimeout", true, "stopNow=never")
   785	            }
   786	        }
   787	        UiDebugLog.action("MainActivity.onStop", "persistSessions=true")
   788	        UiDebugLog.result("MainActivity.onStop", true, "stoppedAllSessions=false")
   789	    }
   790	
   791	    private fun handleIncomingIntent(intent: Intent?) {
   792	        intent?.getStringExtra(EXTRA_START_ROUTE)
   793	            ?.takeIf(::isSupportedStartupRoute)
   794	            ?.let { requestedStartupRoute.value = it }
   795	        when (intent?.action) {
   796	            SessionService.ACTION_OPEN_SESSION -> {
   797	                val hostId = intent.getStringExtra(SessionService.EXTRA_HOST_ID).orEmpty()
   798	                if (hostId.isBlank()) {
   799	                    UiDebugLog.result("handleSessionOpenIntent", false, "missing-host-id")
   800	                    return
   801	                }
   802	                requestedOpenSessionHostId.value = hostId
   803	                requestedOpenSessionFileTransferEntryMode.value = null
   804	                UiDebugLog.result("handleSessionOpenIntent", true, "hostId=$hostId")
   805	            }
   806	
   807	            ACTION_WIDGET_CONNECT -> {
   808	                if (!HostWidgets.isTrustedWidgetActionIntent(this, intent)) {
   809	                    UiDebugLog.result("handleWidgetConnectIntent", false, "untrusted-intent")
   810	                    return
   811	                }
   812	                val hostId = intent.getStringExtra(EXTRA_WIDGET_HOST_ID).orEmpty()
   813	                val mode = runCatching {
   814	                    ConnectionMode.valueOf(intent.getStringExtra(EXTRA_WIDGET_MODE).orEmpty())
   815	                }.getOrDefault(ConnectionMode.SSH)
   816	                val fileTransferEntryMode = intent.getStringExtra(EXTRA_WIDGET_FILE_TRANSFER_ENTRY_MODE)
   817	                    ?.let { value ->
   818	                        runCatching { FileTransferEntryMode.valueOf(value) }.getOrNull()
   819	                    }
   820	                if (hostId.isBlank()) {
   821	                    UiDebugLog.result("handleWidgetConnectIntent", false, "missing-host-id")
   822	                    return
   823	                }
   824	                pendingWidgetConnectHostId.value = hostId
   825	                pendingWidgetConnectMode.value = mode
   826	                pendingWidgetConnectFileTransferEntryMode.value = fileTransferEntryMode
   827	                UiDebugLog.result("handleWidgetConnectIntent", true, "hostId=$hostId, mode=$mode")
   828	            }
   829	        }
   830	    }
   831	
   832	    private fun restorePendingIntentState(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
   833	        if (savedInstanceState == null) return
   834	        requestedOpenSessionHostId.value =
   835	            savedInstanceState.getString(STATE_REQUESTED_OPEN_SESSION_ID)
   836	        requestedStartupRoute.value =
   837	            savedInstanceState.getString(STATE_REQUESTED_STARTUP_ROUTE)
   838	                ?.takeIf(::isSupportedStartupRoute)
   839	        pendingWidgetConnectHostId.value =
   840	            savedInstanceState.getString(STATE_PENDING_WIDGET_HOST_ID)
   841	        pendingWidgetConnectMode.value =
   842	            savedInstanceState.getString(STATE_PENDING_WIDGET_MODE)
   843	                ?.let { value -> runCatching { ConnectionMode.valueOf(value) }.getOrNull() }
   844	        pendingWidgetConnectFileTransferEntryMode.value =
   845	            savedInstanceState.getString(STATE_PENDING_WIDGET_FILE_TRANSFER_ENTRY_MODE)
   846	                ?.let { value -> runCatching { FileTransferEntryMode.valueOf(value) }.getOrNull() }
   847	    }
   848

   850	        Routes.HOME,

   854	        Routes.FORWARDS,

   856	        Routes.KEYBOARD,
   857	        Routes.THEME_EDITOR,
   858	        Routes.SETTINGS,
   859	        Routes.OPEN_SOURCE_LICENSES
   860	    )

    94	/**
    95	 * Foreground service that keeps SSH/Mosh sessions alive.
    96	 * Sessions remain connected until explicitly stopped.
    97	 */
    98	class SessionService : Service() {
    99	
   100	    private val serviceScope = CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.IO)
   101	    private val binder = SessionBinder()
   102	    private val activeJobs = ConcurrentHashMap<String, Job>()
   103	    private val activeConnections = ConcurrentHashMap<String, ActiveConnection>()
   104	    private val runtimeSessionPasswords = ConcurrentHashMap<String, String>()
   105	    private val sessionSnapshots = MutableStateFlow<List<SessionSnapshot>>(emptyList())
   106	    private val hostKeyPrompts = MutableStateFlow<List<HostKeyPrompt>>(emptyList())
   107	    private val hostKeyPromptWaiters = ConcurrentHashMap<String, CompletableFuture<Boolean>>()
   108	    private val passwordPrompts = MutableStateFlow<List<PasswordPrompt>>(emptyList())
   109	    private val passwordPromptWaiters = ConcurrentHashMap<String, CompletableFuture<PasswordResponse>>()
   110	    private val shellOutput = MutableStateFlow<Map<String, String>>(emptyMap())
   111	    private val remoteDirectories = MutableStateFlow<Map<String, RemoteDirectorySnapshot>>(emptyMap())
   112	    private val fileTransferProgress = MutableStateFlow<Map<String, FileTransferProgress>>(emptyMap())
   113	    private val shellSizes = ConcurrentHashMap<String, Pair<Int, Int>>()
   114	    private val shellReadSequence = ConcurrentHashMap<String, Int>()
   115	    private val shellWriteSequence = ConcurrentHashMap<String, Int>()
   116	    private val pendingShellOutputChunks = ConcurrentHashMap<String, StringBuilder>()
   117	    private val pendingShellSnapshots = ConcurrentHashMap<String, String>()
   118	    private val shellOutputPublishJobs = ConcurrentHashMap<String, Job>()
   119	    private val moshTranscriptPublishJobs = ConcurrentHashMap<String, Job>()
   120	    private val moshReconnectJobs = ConcurrentHashMap<String, Job>()
   121	    private val transferNotificationRefreshJobs = ConcurrentHashMap<String, Job>()
   122	    private val notificationStateLock = Any()
   123	    private var foregroundNotificationId: Int? = null
   124	    private val sessionNotificationIds = mutableSetOf<Int>()
   125	    @Volatile
   126	    private var diagnosticsEnabled: Boolean = false
   127	
   128	    override fun onCreate() {
   129	        super.onCreate()
   130	        UiDebugLog.action("SessionService.onCreate")
   131	        serviceScope.launch {
   132	            SettingsStore.diagnosticsEnabled.collect { enabled ->
   133	                diagnosticsEnabled = enabled
   134	            }
   135	        }
   136	        createChannel()
   137	        UiDebugLog.result("SessionService.onCreate", true)
   138	    }
   139	
   140	    override fun onStartCommand(intent: Intent?, flags: Int, startId: Int): Int {
   141	        UiDebugLog.action("SessionService.onStartCommand", "action=${intent?.action}, startId=$startId")
   142	        when (intent?.action) {
   143	            ACTION_STOP_ALL -> stopAllSessions()
   144	            ACTION_STOP_SESSION -> intent.getStringExtra(EXTRA_HOST_ID)?.let { hostId ->
   145	                if (hostId.isNotBlank()) {
   146	                    stopSession(hostId)
   147	                }
   148	            }
   149	        }
   150	        UiDebugLog.result("SessionService.onStartCommand", true)
   151	        return START_STICKY
   152	    }
   153	
   154	    override fun onBind(intent: Intent?): IBinder = binder
   155	
   156	    override fun onDestroy() {
   157	        UiDebugLog.action("SessionService.onDestroy", "activeSessions=${activeJobs.size}")
   158	        super.onDestroy()
   159	        stopAllSessions()
   160	        clearAllHostKeyPrompts()
   161	        clearAllPasswordPrompts()
   162	        runtimeSessionPasswords.clear()
   163	        shellOutputPublishJobs.values.forEach { it.cancel() }
   164	        shellOutputPublishJobs.clear()
   165	        moshTranscriptPublishJobs.values.forEach { it.cancel() }
   166	        moshTranscriptPublishJobs.clear()
   167	        moshReconnectJobs.values.forEach { it.cancel() }
   168	        moshReconnectJobs.clear()
   169	        transferNotificationRefreshJobs.values.forEach { it.cancel() }
   170	        transferNotificationRefreshJobs.clear()
   171	        pendingShellOutputChunks.clear()
   172	        pendingShellSnapshots.clear()
   173	        shellOutput.value = emptyMap()
   174	        remoteDirectories.value = emptyMap()
   175	        fileTransferProgress.value = emptyMap()
   176	        shellSizes.clear()
   177	        shellReadSequence.clear()
   178	        shellWriteSequence.clear()
   179	        UiDebugLog.result("SessionService.onDestroy", true)
   180	    }
   181	
   182	    fun startSession(
   183	        requestedSessionId: String? = null,
   184	        host: HostConnection,
   185	        mode: ConnectionMode,
   186	        passwordOverride: String? = null,
   187	        availableForwards: List<PortForward> = emptyList(),
   188	        availableSnippets: List<Snippet> = emptyList(),
   189	        autoStartForwards: Boolean = true,
   190	        autoTrustUnknownHostKey: Boolean = false,
   191	        hostKeyPromptEnabled: Boolean = true,
   192	        allowPasswordSave: Boolean = false,
   193	        terminalEmulation: TerminalEmulation = TerminalEmulation.XTERM
   194	    ) {
   195	        val resolvedSessionId = requestedSessionId
   196	            ?.trim()
   197	            ?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
   198	            ?: generateSessionId(host.id, mode)
   199	        UiDebugLog.action(
   200	            "startSession",
   201	            "sessionId=$resolvedSessionId, hostId=${host.id}, mode=$mode, alreadyActive=${activeJobs.containsKey(resolvedSessionId)}, hasPasswordOverride=${!passwordOverride.isNullOrBlank()}"
   202	        )
   203	        if (activeJobs.containsKey(resolvedSessionId)) {
   204	            UiDebugLog.result("startSession", false, "already-active sessionId=$resolvedSessionId")
   205	            return
   206	        }
   207	        val sessionId = resolvedSessionId
   208	        val useRuntimePasswordCache = !allowPasswordSave
   209	        if (useRuntimePasswordCache && !passwordOverride.isNullOrBlank()) {
   210	            runtimeSessionPasswords[sessionId] = passwordOverride
   211	        }
   212	        val useMoshTransport = host.useMosh && mode == ConnectionMode.SSH
   213	        val needsPassword = host.preferredAuth != AuthMethod.IDENTITY
   214	        val initialPassword = if (needsPassword) {
   215	            when {
   216	                !passwordOverride.isNullOrBlank() -> passwordOverride
   217	                useRuntimePasswordCache -> runtimeSessionPasswords[sessionId]
   218	                else -> runCatching { SecurityManager.getHostPassword(host.id) }.getOrNull()
   219	            }
   220	        } else {
   221	            null
   222	        }
   223	        val job = serviceScope.launch {
   224	            var client: SSHClient? = null
   225	            var shellBinding: ShellBinding? = null
   226	            var moshBinding: MoshBinding? = null
   227	            var sftpBinding: SftpBinding? = null
   228	            var scpBinding: ScpBinding? = null
   229	            var activeForwardBindings: List<PortForwardBinding> = emptyList()
   230	            var sessionHost = host
   231	            val attemptDeadlineMillis = System.currentTimeMillis() + CONNECTION_ATTEMPT_TIMEOUT_MS
   232	            runCatching {
   233	                updateSessionSnapshot(sessionId, sessionHost, mode, SessionStatus.CONNECTING, "Opening SSH connection...")
   234	                client = SshClientProvider.createClient(
   235	                    this@SessionService,
   236	                    sessionHost,
   237	                    SessionLoggerFactory(sessionId),
   238	                    autoTrustUnknownHostKey = autoTrustUnknownHostKey,
   239	                    onHostKeyPrompt = if (hostKeyPromptEnabled) {
   240	                        { prompt ->
   241	                            awaitHostKeyDecision(sessionId, prompt, attemptDeadlineMillis)
   242	                        }
   243	                    } else {
   244	                        null
   245	                    }
   246	                )
   247	                throwIfAttemptTimedOut(attemptDeadlineMillis)
   248	                client!!.connect(host.host, host.port)
   249	                throwIfAttemptTimedOut(attemptDeadlineMillis)
   250	                when (host.preferredAuth) {
   251	                    AuthMethod.PASSWORD -> {
   252	                        authenticateWithPassword(
   253	                            client = client!!,
   254	                            host = host,
   255	                            sessionId = sessionId,
   256	                            mode = mode,
   257	                            initialPassword = initialPassword,
   258	                            deadlineMillis = attemptDeadlineMillis,
   259	                            allowPasswordSave = allowPasswordSave,
   260	                            useRuntimePasswordCache = useRuntimePasswordCache

hook: PreToolUse Completed
exec
/usr/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba app/src/main/java/com/sshpeaches/app/service/SessionService.kt | sed -n '260,620p'" in /home/zenulabidin/Documents/sshpeaches
 succeeded in 0ms:
   260	                            useRuntimePasswordCache = useRuntimePasswordCache
   261	                        )
   262	                    }
   263	                    AuthMethod.IDENTITY -> {
   264	                        authenticateWithIdentity(
   265	                            client = client!!,
   266	                            host = host,
   267	                            sessionId = sessionId,
   268	                            mode = mode,
   269	                            required = true
   270	                        )
   271	                    }
   272	                    AuthMethod.PASSWORD_AND_IDENTITY -> {
   273	                        authenticateWithIdentity(
   274	                            client = client!!,
   275	                            host = host,
   276	                            sessionId = sessionId,
   277	                            mode = mode,
   278	                            required = false
   279	                        )
   280	                        if (!client!!.isAuthenticated) {
   281	                            authenticateWithPassword(
   282	                                client = client!!,
   283	                                host = host,
   284	                                sessionId = sessionId,
   285	                                mode = mode,
   286	                                initialPassword = initialPassword,
   287	                                deadlineMillis = attemptDeadlineMillis,
   288	                                allowPasswordSave = allowPasswordSave,
   289	                                useRuntimePasswordCache = useRuntimePasswordCache
   290	                            )
   291	                        }
   292	                    }
   293	                }
   294	                detectRemoteOsMetadata(sessionId, client!!)?.let { detected ->
   295	                    if (sessionHost.osMetadata != detected) {
   296	                        sessionHost = sessionHost.copy(osMetadata = detected)
   297	                        SessionLogBus.emit(
   298	                            SessionLogBus.Entry(
   299	                                hostId = sessionId,
   300	                                level = SessionLogBus.LogLevel.INFO,
   301	                                message = "Detected remote OS: ${detected.toSummaryLabel()}"
   302	                            )
   303	                        )
   304	                    }
   305	                }
   306	                when (mode) {
   307	                    ConnectionMode.SSH -> {
   308	                        if (useMoshTransport) {
   309	                            updateSessionSnapshot(sessionId, sessionHost, mode, SessionStatus.CONNECTING, "Starting mosh-server...")
   310	                            val moshConnect = startMoshServer(sessionId, client!!)
   311	                            throwIfAttemptTimedOut(attemptDeadlineMillis)
   312	                            updateSessionSnapshot(sessionId, sessionHost, mode, SessionStatus.CONNECTING, "Starting mosh client...")
   313	                            moshBinding = startMoshClient(
   314	                                hostId = sessionId,
   315	                                host = sessionHost,
   316	                                moshConnect = moshConnect,
   317	                                terminalEmulation = terminalEmulation
   318	                            )
   319	                        } else {
   320	                            updateSessionSnapshot(sessionId, sessionHost, mode, SessionStatus.CONNECTING, "Starting shell...")
   321	                            shellBinding = openShell(sessionId, client!!, terminalEmulation)
   322	                        }
   323	                    }
   324	                    ConnectionMode.SFTP -> {
   325	                        updateSessionSnapshot(sessionId, sessionHost, mode, SessionStatus.CONNECTING, "Opening SFTP subsystem...")
   326	                        sftpBinding = SftpBinding(client!!.newSFTPClient())
   327	                        refreshSftpDirectoryListing(sessionId, sftpBinding!!.client, ".")
   328	                    }
   329	                    ConnectionMode.SCP -> {
   330	                        updateSessionSnapshot(sessionId, sessionHost, mode, SessionStatus.CONNECTING, "Preparing file browser...")
   331	                        sftpBinding = SftpBinding(client!!.newSFTPClient())
   332	                        scpBinding = ScpBinding(client!!.newSCPFileTransfer())
   333	                        refreshSftpDirectoryListing(sessionId, sftpBinding!!.client, ".")
   334	                        SessionLogBus.emit(
   335	                            SessionLogBus.Entry(
   336	                                hostId = sessionId,
   337	                                level = SessionLogBus.LogLevel.INFO,
   338	                                message = "File browser ready. Browse remote files and download them."
   339	                            )
   340	                        )
   341	                    }
   342	                }
   343	                val startupCommand = resolveStartupCommand(host.startupScript, availableSnippets)
   344	                if (startupCommand.isNotBlank() && mode == ConnectionMode.SSH) {
   345	                    runCatching {
   346	                        client!!.startSession().use { shell ->
   347	                            val cmd = shell.exec(startupCommand)
   348	                            cmd.join(12, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
   349	                            val output = runCatching { cmd.inputStream.bufferedReader().readText() }.getOrNull()
   350	                            if (!output.isNullOrBlank()) {
   351	                                SessionLogBus.emit(
   352	                                    SessionLogBus.Entry(
   353	                                        hostId = sessionId,
   354	                                        level = SessionLogBus.LogLevel.INFO,
   355	                                        message = output.trim()
   356	                                    )
   357	                                )
   358	                            }
   359	                        }
   360	                    }.onFailure { err ->
   361	                        SessionLogBus.emit(
   362	                            SessionLogBus.Entry(
   363	                                hostId = sessionId,
   364	                                level = SessionLogBus.LogLevel.WARN,
   365	                                message = "Startup script failed: ${err.message ?: "unknown error"}"
   366	                            )
   367	                        )
   368	                    }
   369	                } else if (mode == ConnectionMode.SSH &&
   370	                    parseSnippetReference(host.startupScript) != null &&
   371	                    host.startupScript.isNotBlank()
   372	                ) {
   373	                    SessionLogBus.emit(
   374	                        SessionLogBus.Entry(
   375	                            hostId = sessionId,
   376	                            level = SessionLogBus.LogLevel.WARN,
   377	                            message = "Startup snippet is missing; skipped startup command."
   378	                        )
   379	                    )
   380	                }
   381	                val configuredForwards = if (autoStartForwards) {
   382	                    availableForwards.filter { it.enabled && it.selectedHostId() == host.id }
   383	                } else {
   384	                    emptyList()
   385	                }
   386	                if (configuredForwards.isNotEmpty()) {
   387	                    updateSessionSnapshot(sessionId, sessionHost, mode, SessionStatus.CONNECTING, "Starting ${configuredForwards.size} port forward(s)...")
   388	                    activeForwardBindings = activatePortForwards(
   389	                        hostId = sessionId,
   390	                        client = client!!,
   391	                        sessionHost = sessionHost,
   392	                        forwards = configuredForwards
   393	                    )
   394	                }
   395	                activeConnections[sessionId] = ActiveConnection(
   396	                    host = sessionHost,
   397	                    mode = mode,
   398	                    client = client,
   399	                    shellBinding = shellBinding,
   400	                    moshBinding = moshBinding,
   401	                    sftpBinding = sftpBinding,
   402	                    scpBinding = scpBinding,
   403	                    portForwardBindings = activeForwardBindings
   404	                )
   405	                val modeLabel = when (mode) {
   406	                    ConnectionMode.SSH -> if (useMoshTransport) "Mosh session ready" else "Interactive shell session ready"
   407	                    ConnectionMode.SFTP -> "SFTP browser ready"
   408	                    ConnectionMode.SCP -> "File transfer ready"
   409	                }
   410	                updateSessionSnapshot(sessionId, sessionHost, mode, SessionStatus.ACTIVE, modeLabel)
   411	                UiDebugLog.result("startSession", true, "sessionId=$sessionId, mode=$mode")
   412	
   413	                // Keep the connection alive until user stops it.
   414	                while (currentCoroutineContext().isActive) {
   415	                    delay(10_000)
   416	                }
   417	            }.onFailure { e ->
   418	                if (e !is CancellationException) {
   419	                    clearHostKeyPromptsForHost(sessionId, trust = false)
   420	                    clearPasswordPromptsForHost(sessionId, password = null)
   421	                    val statusMessage = e.message ?: "Connection failed"
   422	                    updateSessionSnapshot(sessionId, sessionHost, mode, SessionStatus.ERROR, statusMessage)
   423	                    UiDebugLog.error("startSession", e, "sessionId=$sessionId, mode=$mode")
   424	                    UiDebugLog.result("startSession", false, "sessionId=$sessionId, mode=$mode")
   425	                }
   426	            }
   427	            runCatching { shellBinding?.inputQueue?.close() }
   428	            runCatching { shellBinding?.inputWriterJob?.cancel() }
   429	            runCatching { shellBinding?.shell?.close() }
   430	            runCatching { shellBinding?.session?.close() }
   431	            runCatching { moshBinding?.inputQueue?.close() }
   432	            runCatching { moshBinding?.inputWriterJob?.cancel() }
   433	            runCatching { moshBinding?.session?.finishIfRunning() }
   434	            runCatching { sftpBinding?.client?.close() }
   435	            runCatching { activeForwardBindings.forEach { closePortForwardBinding(it) } }
   436	            runCatching { client?.disconnect() }
   437	            activeConnections.remove(sessionId)
   438	            clearHostKeyPromptsForHost(sessionId, trust = false)
   439	            clearPasswordPromptsForHost(sessionId, password = null)
   440	            clearRuntimeSessionPassword(sessionId)
   441	            clearShellOutputForHost(sessionId)
   442	            clearRemoteDirectoryForHost(sessionId)
   443	            clearFileTransferProgress(sessionId)
   444	        }
   445	        job.invokeOnCompletion {
   446	            activeJobs.remove(sessionId)
   447	            activeConnections.remove(sessionId)
   448	            clearRuntimeSessionPassword(sessionId)
   449	            clearFileTransferProgress(sessionId)
   450	            updateSessionNotifications()
   451	            if (it is CancellationException) {
   452	                removeSessionSnapshot(sessionId)
   453	            }
   454	        }
   455	        activeJobs[sessionId] = job
   456	        updateSessionNotifications()
   457	    }
   458	
   459	    fun stopSession(hostId: String) {
   460	        UiDebugLog.action("stopSession", "hostId=$hostId")
   461	        clearHostKeyPromptsForHost(hostId, trust = false)
   462	        clearPasswordPromptsForHost(hostId, password = null)
   463	        clearRuntimeSessionPassword(hostId)
   464	        moshReconnectJobs.remove(hostId)?.cancel()
   465	        val connection = activeConnections.remove(hostId)
   466	        connection?.let {
   467	            serviceScope.launch {
   468	                closeConnectionResources(hostId, it, trigger = "stopSession")
   469	            }
   470	        }
   471	        activeJobs.remove(hostId)?.cancel()
   472	        updateSessionNotifications()
   473	        removeSessionSnapshot(hostId)
   474	        clearShellOutputForHost(hostId)
   475	        clearRemoteDirectoryForHost(hostId)
   476	        clearFileTransferProgress(hostId)
   477	        shellReadSequence.remove(hostId)
   478	        shellWriteSequence.remove(hostId)
   479	        UiDebugLog.result("stopSession", true, "hostId=$hostId")
   480	    }
   481	
   482	    fun hasActivePortForwards(): Boolean =
   483	        activeConnections.values.any { it.portForwardBindings.isNotEmpty() }
   484	
   485	    fun stopAllSessions() {
   486	        UiDebugLog.action("stopAllSessions", "count=${activeJobs.size}")
   487	        val ids = activeJobs.keys.toList()
   488	        ids.forEach { stopSession(it) }
   489	        UiDebugLog.result("stopAllSessions", true)
   490	    }
   491	
   492	    private fun closeConnectionResources(hostId: String, connection: ActiveConnection, trigger: String) {
   493	        measureOperation("closeConnectionResources/$trigger", hostId, thresholdMs = 200L) {
   494	            runCatching { connection.shellBinding?.inputQueue?.close() }
   495	            runCatching { connection.shellBinding?.inputWriterJob?.cancel() }
   496	            runCatching { connection.shellBinding?.shell?.close() }
   497	            runCatching { connection.shellBinding?.session?.close() }
   498	            runCatching { connection.moshBinding?.inputQueue?.close() }
   499	            runCatching { connection.moshBinding?.inputWriterJob?.cancel() }
   500	            runCatching { connection.moshBinding?.session?.finishIfRunning() }
   501	            runCatching { connection.sftpBinding?.client?.close() }
   502	            runCatching { connection.portForwardBindings.forEach { closePortForwardBinding(it) } }
   503	            runCatching { connection.client?.disconnect() }
   504	        }
   505	    }
   506	
   507	    fun sendKeyboardShortcut(hostId: String, shortcut: String) {
   508	        UiDebugLog.action("sendKeyboardShortcut", "hostId=$hostId, shortcutBlank=${shortcut.isBlank()}")
   509	        if (shortcut.isBlank()) {
   510	            UiDebugLog.result("sendKeyboardShortcut", false, "blank-shortcut")
   511	            return
   512	        }
   513	        val connection = activeConnections[hostId]
   514	        if (connection == null) {
   515	            UiDebugLog.result("sendKeyboardShortcut", false, "session-not-active hostId=$hostId")
   516	            return
   517	        }
   518	        if (connection.mode != ConnectionMode.SSH) {
   519	            SessionLogBus.emit(
   520	                SessionLogBus.Entry(
   521	                    hostId = hostId,
   522	                    level = SessionLogBus.LogLevel.WARN,
   523	                    message = "Snippet/shortcut execution is only available in SSH mode."
   524	                )
   525	            )
   526	            UiDebugLog.result("sendKeyboardShortcut", false, "unsupported-mode hostId=$hostId")
   527	            return
   528	        }
   529	        val payload = shortcut.toShellCommandPayload()
   530	        serviceScope.launch {
   531	            runCatching {
   532	                if (connection.moshBinding != null || connection.shellBinding != null) {
   533	                    sendShellBytes(hostId, payload.toByteArray(StandardCharsets.UTF_8))
   534	                } else {
   535	                    val client = connection.client ?: error("No active SSH client")
   536	                    client.startSession().use { session ->
   537	                        val command = session.exec(shortcut)
   538	                        command.join(8, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
   539	                        val output = runCatching { command.inputStream.bufferedReader().readText() }.getOrNull()
   540	                        if (!output.isNullOrBlank()) {
   541	                            SessionLogBus.emit(
   542	                                SessionLogBus.Entry(
   543	                                    hostId = hostId,
   544	                                    level = SessionLogBus.LogLevel.INFO,
   545	                                    message = output.trim()
   546	                                )
   547	                            )
   548	                        }
   549	                    }
   550	                }
   551	            }.onSuccess {
   552	                SessionLogBus.emit(
   553	                    SessionLogBus.Entry(
   554	                        hostId = hostId,
   555	                        level = SessionLogBus.LogLevel.INFO,
   556	                        message = "Executed: $shortcut"
   557	                    )
   558	                )
   559	                UiDebugLog.result("sendKeyboardShortcut", true, "hostId=$hostId")
   560	            }.onFailure { error ->
   561	                SessionLogBus.emit(
   562	                    SessionLogBus.Entry(
   563	                        hostId = hostId,
   564	                        level = SessionLogBus.LogLevel.ERROR,
   565	                        message = error.message ?: "Command failed"
   566	                    )
   567	                )
   568	                UiDebugLog.result("sendKeyboardShortcut", false, "hostId=$hostId")
   569	            }
   570	        }
   571	    }
   572	
   573	    private fun String.toShellCommandPayload(): String {
   574	        val trimmed = trimEnd()
   575	        if (trimmed.isEmpty()) return this
   576	        return if (trimmed.contains('\n') || trimmed.contains('\r')) this else "$trimmed\r"
   577	    }
   578	
   579	    fun sessionsFlow(): StateFlow<List<SessionSnapshot>> = sessionSnapshots.asStateFlow()
   580	    fun hostKeyPromptsFlow(): StateFlow<List<HostKeyPrompt>> = hostKeyPrompts.asStateFlow()
   581	    fun passwordPromptsFlow(): StateFlow<List<PasswordPrompt>> = passwordPrompts.asStateFlow()
   582	    fun shellOutputFlow(): StateFlow<Map<String, String>> = shellOutput.asStateFlow()
   583	    fun remoteDirectoryFlow(): StateFlow<Map<String, RemoteDirectorySnapshot>> = remoteDirectories.asStateFlow()
   584	    fun fileTransferProgressFlow(): StateFlow<Map<String, FileTransferProgress>> = fileTransferProgress.asStateFlow()
   585	    fun resolveTerminalEmulator(hostId: String): TerminalEmulator? {
   586	        val connection = activeConnections[hostId] ?: return null
   587	        return connection.shellBinding?.terminalEngine?.emulator()
   588	            ?: connection.moshBinding?.session?.emulator
   589	    }
   590	
   591	    fun resolveMoshTerminalEmulator(hostId: String): TerminalEmulator? {
   592	        return resolveTerminalEmulator(hostId)
   593	    }
   594	
   595	    fun respondToHostKeyPrompt(promptId: String, trust: Boolean) {
   596	        hostKeyPromptWaiters.remove(promptId)?.complete(trust)
   597	        hostKeyPrompts.value = hostKeyPrompts.value.filterNot { it.id == promptId }
   598	    }
   599	
   600	    fun respondToPasswordPrompt(promptId: String, password: String?, savePassword: Boolean) {
   601	        val prompt = passwordPrompts.value.firstOrNull { it.id == promptId }
   602	        if (prompt?.allowSave == false && !password.isNullOrBlank()) {
   603	            runtimeSessionPasswords[prompt.hostId] = password
   604	        }
   605	        passwordPromptWaiters.remove(promptId)?.complete(
   606	            PasswordResponse(
   607	                password = password,
   608	                savePassword = savePassword
   609	            )
   610	        )
   611	        passwordPrompts.value = passwordPrompts.value.filterNot { it.id == promptId }
   612	    }
   613	
   614	    fun getRuntimeSessionPassword(sessionId: String): String? = runtimeSessionPasswords[sessionId]
   615	
   616	    fun moveRuntimeSessionPassword(fromSessionId: String, toSessionId: String) {
   617	        if (fromSessionId == toSessionId) return
   618	        val password = runtimeSessionPasswords.remove(fromSessionId) ?: return
   619	        runtimeSessionPasswords[toSessionId] = password
   620	    }

   678	    private fun waitForForwardedHttpResponse(forwardedPort: Int, timeoutMillis: Long = 20_000): String {
   679	        val deadline = System.currentTimeMillis() + timeoutMillis
   680	        var lastError: Throwable? = null
   681	        while (System.currentTimeMillis() < deadline) {
   682	            try {
   683	                Socket().use { socket ->
   684	                    socket.connect(InetSocketAddress("127.0.0.1", forwardedPort), 2_000)
   685	                    socket.soTimeout = 2_000
   686	                    val writer = socket.getOutputStream().bufferedWriter()
   687	                    writer.apply {
   688	                        writer.write("GET /health HTTP/1.1\r\n")
   689	                        writer.write("Host: 127.0.0.1\r\n")
   690	                        writer.write("Connection: close\r\n")
   691	                        writer.write("\r\n")
   692	                        writer.flush()
   693	                    }
   694	                    val buffer = ByteArray(1_024)
   695	                    val response = StringBuilder()
   696	                    val input = socket.getInputStream()
   697	                    while (true) {
   698	                        val read = input.read(buffer)
   699	                        if (read < 0) break
   700	                        response.append(String(buffer, 0, read))
   701	                        if (response.contains("SSHPEACHES_FORWARD_OK")) {
   702	                            return response.toString()
   703	                        }
   704	                    }
   705	                    return response.toString()
   706	                }
   707	            } catch (error: Throwable) {
   708	                lastError = error
   709	                Thread.sleep(500)
   710	            }
   711	        }
   712	        throw AssertionError("Timed out waiting for forwarded HTTP response", lastError)
   713	    }
   714	
   715	}

    18	    AndroidComposeTestRule<ActivityScenarioRule<MainActivity>, MainActivity>
