    20	fun MainActivityComposeRule.resetAppState() {
    21	    AppStateResetter.reset(activity.applicationContext)
    22	    activityRule.scenario.recreate()
    23	    waitForIdle()
    24	}
    25	
    26	fun MainActivityComposeRule.dismissSoftKeyboard() {
    27	    waitForIdle()
    28	    runCatching { Espresso.closeSoftKeyboard() }
    29	    waitForIdle()
    30	}
    31	
    32	fun MainActivityComposeRule.clickNodeWithTag(tag: String, useUnmergedTree: Boolean = false) {
    33	    val node = onNodeWithTag(tag, useUnmergedTree = useUnmergedTree)
    34	    runCatching {
    35	        node.performScrollTo()
    36	        waitForIdle()
    37	    }
    38	    node.performClick()
    39	    waitForIdle()
    40	}
    41	
    42	fun MainActivityComposeRule.openDrawer() {
    43	    val drawerMatcher = hasTestTag(UiTestTags.DRAWER_SCROLL_CONTAINER)
    44	    if (runCatching {
    45	            onNode(drawerMatcher, useUnmergedTree = true).assertIsDisplayed()
    46	        }.isSuccess
    47	    ) {
    48	        return
    49	    }
    50	    onNodeWithContentDescription("Menu").assertIsDisplayed().performClick()
    51	    waitForIdle()
    52	}
    53	
    54	fun MainActivityComposeRule.navigateDrawer(route: String) {
    55	    openDrawer()
    56	    revealDrawerNode(UiTestTags.drawerItem(route))
    57	    onNodeWithTag(UiTestTags.drawerItem(route), useUnmergedTree = true)
    58	        .performClick()
    59	    waitForIdle()
    60	}
    61	
    62	fun MainActivityComposeRule.openQuickConnect() {
    63	    onNodeWithTag(UiTestTags.DRAWER_QUICK_CONNECT)
    64	        .assertIsDisplayed()
    65	        .performClick()
    66	    waitUntil(15_000) {
    67	        runCatching {
    68	            onNodeWithTag(UiTestTags.QUICK_CONNECT_CONNECT_BUTTON).assertIsDisplayed()
    69	            true
    70	        }.getOrDefault(false)
    71	    }
    72	}
    73	
    74	fun MainActivityComposeRule.openSettingsCategory(categoryTitle: String) {
    75	    val categoryTag = UiTestTags.settingsCategory(categoryTitle)
    76	    val categoryExists = onAllNodesWithTag(categoryTag, useUnmergedTree = true)
    77	        .fetchSemanticsNodes()
    78	        .isNotEmpty()
    79	    if (!categoryExists) return
    80	
    81	    onNodeWithTag(categoryTag, useUnmergedTree = true)
    82	        .assertIsDisplayed()
    83	        .performClick()
    84	    waitForIdle()
    85	}
    86	
    87	fun MainActivityComposeRule.revealSettingsControl(
    88	    tag: String,
    89	    categoryTitle: String? = settingsCategoryForControl(tag)
    90	) {
    91	    categoryTitle?.let(::openSettingsCategory)
    92	    waitForIdle()
    93	    repeat(16) {
    94	        val revealed = runCatching {
    95	            onNodeWithTag(tag, useUnmergedTree = true).performScrollTo()
    96	            waitForIdle()
    97	            true
    98	        }.getOrDefault(false)
    99	        val visible = runCatching {
   100	            onNodeWithTag(tag, useUnmergedTree = true).assertIsDisplayed()
   101	            true
   102	        }.getOrDefault(false)
   103	        if (revealed && visible) return
   104	        if (visible) return
   105	        runCatching {
   106	            onNodeWithTag(UiTestTags.SETTINGS_SCROLL_CONTAINER, useUnmergedTree = true)
   107	                .performTouchInput { swipeUp() }
   108	        }.getOrElse {
   109	            onNodeWithTag(UiTestTags.SCREEN_SETTINGS, useUnmergedTree = true)
   110	                .performTouchInput { swipeUp() }
   111	        }
   112	        waitForIdle()
   113	    }
   114	    onNodeWithTag(tag, useUnmergedTree = true).assertIsDisplayed()
   115	}
   116	
   117	private fun MainActivityComposeRule.revealDrawerNode(tag: String) {
   118	    val target = onNodeWithTag(tag, useUnmergedTree = true)
   119	    runCatching {
   120	        target.performScrollTo()
   121	        waitForIdle()
   122	        target.assertIsDisplayed()
   123	        return
   124	    }
   125	    repeat(5) {
   126	        val shown = runCatching {
   127	            target.assertIsDisplayed()
   128	            true
   129	        }.getOrDefault(false)
   130	        if (shown) {
   131	            return
   132	        }
   133	        onNodeWithTag(UiTestTags.DRAWER_SCROLL_CONTAINER, useUnmergedTree = true)
   134	            .performTouchInput { swipeUp() }
   135	        waitForIdle()
   136	    }
   137	    target.assertIsDisplayed()
   138	}
   139	
   140	private fun settingsCategoryForControl(tag: String): String? = when (tag) {
   141	    UiTestTags.SETTINGS_THEME_MODE_FIELD -> "Appearance"
   142	    UiTestTags.SETTINGS_BACKGROUND_SWITCH -> "Background"
   143	    UiTestTags.SETTINGS_TERMINAL_EMULATION_FIELD,
   144	    UiTestTags.SETTINGS_TERMINAL_BELL_FIELD,
   145	    UiTestTags.SETTINGS_TERMINAL_VOLUME_BUTTONS_SWITCH,
   146	    UiTestTags.SETTINGS_TERMINAL_MARGIN_INPUT,
   147	    UiTestTags.SETTINGS_MOSH_SERVER_COMMAND_INPUT,
   148	    UiTestTags.SETTINGS_BUILTIN_KEYBOARD_SWITCH -> "Terminal"
   149	    UiTestTags.SETTINGS_BIOMETRIC_SWITCH,
   150	    UiTestTags.SETTINGS_PIN_STATUS_TEXT,
   151	    UiTestTags.SETTINGS_SET_PIN_BUTTON,
   152	    UiTestTags.SETTINGS_DISABLE_PIN_BUTTON,
   153	    UiTestTags.SETTINGS_HOST_KEY_PROMPT_SWITCH,
   154	    UiTestTags.SETTINGS_AUTO_TRUST_HOST_KEY_SWITCH -> "Security"
   155	    UiTestTags.SETTINGS_DIAGNOSTICS_SWITCH -> "Diagnostics"
   156	    UiTestTags.SETTINGS_EXPORT_QR_BUTTON -> "Transfer / QR"
   157	    UiTestTags.SETTINGS_RESTORE_DEFAULTS_BUTTON -> "Advanced"
   158	    else -> null
   159	}

codex
The stale path is in `SSHPeachesRoot`: the nav route can remain `SESSION`/`CONNECTING` while `quickConnectRequest` still names an old session, but the service’s `sessions` list has already dropped it and `resolveTerminalEmulator(id)` would return null. I’m adding a route-level guard keyed on “service is bound and the requested session has no snapshot,” so it runs for resume, launcher relaunch, notification intents, and task restore without changing live-session restoration.

hook: PreToolUse Completed

codex
The guard also suppresses the session composable for the stale frame, so `ConnectingScreen` and its terminal view are not composed while navigation is being corrected. I’m now passing an explicit service-ready signal and switching session collection to read the `StateFlow`’s current value immediately.

Fixed by passing sessionServiceReady into SSHPeachesRoot, reading SessionService StateFlows with their current values, detecting missing backed sessions on ready service, suppressing stale SessionVerticalContent, and navigating immediately back to routeBeforeConnecting/Home.
